<!--TgQPHd|||[[&quot;python&quot;,&quot;import os\nimport sys\nfrom openai import OpenAI\n\n# Initialize the client\n# The OpenAI API strictly protects data privacy; inputs are not used for model training.\nclient \u003d OpenAI(api_key\u003dos.getenv(\&quot;OPENAI_API_KEY\&quot;))\n\nif not os.getenv(\&quot;OPENAI_API_KEY\&quot;):\n    print(\&quot;❌ Error: OPENAI_API_KEY environment variable not found.\&quot;)\n    sys.exit(1)\n\n# Define the specialized system engines\nENGINES \u003d {\n    \&quot;WEB\&quot;: \&quot;You are a Linux Web Server Expert (Nginx/Apache). Parse traffic anomalies, 4xx/5xx errors, and upstream timeouts. Provide safe systemctl reloads and configuration hotfixes.\&quot;,\n    \&quot;DOCKER\&quot;: \&quot;You are a Container Runtime Expert (Docker/Containerd). Parse container crashes, exit codes (like OOM 137), volume permissions, and network bridge conflicts.\&quot;,\n    \&quot;K8S\&quot;: \&quot;You are a Kubernetes Orchestration Expert. Parse kubectl describe pod outputs, CrashLoopBackOff states, ImagePullBackOff, and PVC binding failures. Suggest safe kubectl controls.\&quot;,\n    \&quot;SYSTEM\&quot;: \&quot;You are an Enterprise Security \u0026 Syslog Expert. Parse syslog, dmesg, and auth.log. Identify hardware faults, SSH brute-force, and kernel panics. Suggest secure iptables/ufw or systemd fixes.\&quot;\n}\n\ndef route_and_parse_log(log_contents):\n    print(\&quot;🔎 Step 1: Routing Log to specialized engine...\&quot;)\n    \n    # Router Prompt to classify the incoming text data safely\n    router_prompt \u003d (\n        \&quot;Analyze the following raw log snippet. Classify it into exactly one of these categories: \&quot;\n        \&quot;WEB, DOCKER, K8S, SYSTEM. Respond with ONLY the category name word, nothing else.\&quot;\n    )\n    \n    try:\n        # Route classification\n        router_response \u003d client.chat.completions.create(\n            model\u003d\&quot;gpt-4o-mini\&quot;,\n            messages\u003d[\n                {\&quot;role\&quot;: \&quot;system\&quot;, \&quot;content\&quot;: router_prompt},\n                {\&quot;role\&quot;: \&quot;user\&quot;, \&quot;content\&quot;: log_contents}\n            ],\n            temperature\u003d0.0\n        )\n        \n        category \u003d router_response.choices[0].message.content.strip().upper()\n        if category not in ENGINES:\n            category \u003d \&quot;SYSTEM\&quot; # Default fallback boundary\n            \n        print(print(f\&quot;✅ Routed successfully to Engine: [ {category} ]\\n\&quot;))\n        print(\&quot;🧠 Step 2: Generating safe remediation markdown...\&quot;)\n        \n        # Structure the ultimate output format requirements\n        output_schema \u003d (\n            \&quot;\\n\\nFormat your response strictly using this Markdown template:\\n\&quot;\n            \&quot;### 🚨 Tier 1 Log Diagnosis Report\\n\&quot;\n            \&quot;**1. Incident Overview**\\n* **Detected Domain:** [Domain]\\n* **Severity Level:** [Low/Med/High]\\n* **Root Cause Summary:** [Summary]\\n\\n\&quot;\n            \&quot;**2.
